FMSuperScout kit-color investigation dump — 9/12/2026 6:40:57 AM
Resolved via reverse-engineering: club colors are located in the Club Info sub-object (cluo.Cino).
  club + 0x0C = Unique ID (objo.Duni)
  club + 0xB0 = Pointer to Club Info (cluo.Cino)
  [club + 0xB0] + 0xA8 = Background / Primary shirt color (cluo.Tbcl, 32-bit BGRA)
  [club + 0xB0] + 0xA0 = Foreground / Secondary trim color (cluo.Ttcl, 32-bit BGRA)
